{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"darts","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A pointed missile weapon, intended to be thrown by the hand; for example, a short lance or javelin."]},{"glosses":["Any sharp-pointed missile weapon, such as an arrow."]},{"glosses":["Anything resembling such a missile; something that pierces or wounds like such a weapon."],"tags":["figuratively","sometimes"]},{"glosses":["A small object with a pointed tip at one end and feathers at the other, which is thrown at a target in the game of darts."]},{"glosses":["A concave quadrilateral similar in shape to a chevron or arrowhead."],"topics":["geometry","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A cigarette."],"tags":["Australia","Canada","New-Zealand","colloquial"]},{"glosses":["A dart-shaped target towed behind an aircraft to train shooters."],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]},{"glosses":["A plan or scheme."],"tags":["Australia","obsolete"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Soon as I felt the floor tremor I made a dart for the door.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A sudden or fast movement."]},{"glosses":["A fold that is stitched on a garment."],"topics":["business","manufacturing","sewing","textiles"]},{"glosses":["A dace (fish) (Leuciscus leuciscus)."]},{"glosses":["Any of various species of hesperiid butterfly."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɑːt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"därt","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/dɑɹt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[daɹt̚]"},{"ipa":"[daɹɾ̥]"}],"upstream_line":30822,"upstream_offset":208449123,"upstream_record_sha256":"888480ad4ff7d3df87832536315d68e9d22d6f0b00ba085bd25b25bf4dee6a80","word":"dart"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"darts","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"darting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"darted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"darted","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To throw with a sudden effort or thrust; to hurl or launch."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"As the sun darted forth his beams, she darted a meaningful glance at me.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To send forth suddenly or rapidly; to emit; to shoot."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They had to dart the animal to get close enough to help","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To shoot with a dart, especially a tranquilizer dart."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The flying man darted eastward.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fly or pass swiftly, like a dart; to move rapidly in one direction; to shoot out quickly."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The deer darted from the thicket.","type":"example"},{"text":"The fish darted under a stone.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To start and run with speed; to shoot rapidly along."],"synonyms":[{"word":"dash"},{"word":"steam"}],"tags":["intransitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɑːt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"därt","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/dɑɹt/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[daɹt̚]"},{"ipa":"[daɹɾ̥]"}],"upstream_line":30823,"upstream_offset":208474649,"upstream_record_sha256":"66a3afcd88794b8426fe657f9c094338928c0f86c7a306d45cd7414dc32f8a87","word":"dart"}

{"forms":[{"form":"mercuries","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"mercurie","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Senses relating to the metal.","A silvery-coloured, metallic chemical element with the atomic number 80; it is liquid at room temperature, and toxic."],"synonyms":[{"word":"mul:Hg"},{"word":"mul:☿"},{"word":"azoth"},{"word":"hydrargyrum"},{"word":"quicksilver"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The mercury there has averaged 37.6°C, 2.3°C above the February norm.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Senses relating to the metal.","Preceded by the: mercury (noun sense 1) as used in the column of a barometer or thermometer, its fall or rise thus indicating the decrease or increase of ambient pressure or temperature."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to the metal.","A preparation containing mercury (noun sense 1), especially calomel (mercurous chloride) or corrosive sublimate (mercuric chloride), formerly used as a medicine to treat syphilis, etc."],"tags":["countable","historical","uncountable"],"topics":["medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to the metal.","One of the elemental principles formerly thought to be present in all metals."],"tags":["countable","historical","uncountable"],"topics":["sciences"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to the metal.","Liveliness, volatility."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to plants.","An annual plant, now generally called annual mercury (Mercurialis annua), chiefly native to central and southern Europe which was formerly grown for its medicinal properties; French mercury, herb mercury."],"synonyms":[{"word":"mercurial"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to plants.","Chiefly with a descriptive word.","Any of several plants of the genus Mercurialis; specifically (obsolete), dog's mercury or wild mercury (Mercurialis perennis)."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Senses relating to plants.","Chiefly with a descriptive word.","Any of several plants resembling Mercurialis plants but of a different genus, or having similar medicinal or toxic qualities as annual mercury or dog's mercury.","Allgood or Good King Henry (Blitum bonus-henricus), a species of goosefoot native to central and southern Europe formerly cultivated as a vegetable but now generally regarded as a weed;
